# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-27)

## Corpus Check
- 7180 files · ~17,272,182 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 533 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .log 399, (none) 41, .css 16)

## Summary
- 71837 nodes · 211120 edges · 1588 communities (1434 shown, 154 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8785 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `5c6d6df8`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
